The shortage of funds was not due to any deliverate reckless spending on my part, but because I was not earning enough at the time to pay for my basic living costs. A friend of mind heard of my circumstances and offered to lend me some money, interest free, so that I could make it to the end of that month without having more financial problems. I gratefully accepted the offer and was able to pay all of my outgoings and did not fall into debt. The friend had not stipulated when the borrowed  money was to be paid back. The friend then moved away from the area with his work. I made no effort whatsoever to try and contact him, even although I knew the loan was still outstanding and he had been a true friend to help me out financially when I needed it most. After a few months, a shared colleague of both me and my lender, mentioned that he had seen my friend and he had enquired as to when I would be repaying the debt to him. I chose to ignore this prompt and, to date, I have not repaid the loan. I do feel guilty about still owing the loan, but I feel that my friend could afford not being repaid, as he has a much better salary than me and he has not tried to contact me direct at any stage for repayment. My salary has not improved since getting the loan, so I try and convince myself that his greater salary means he will not miss the money that he lent me.
